- **lazy-commit-guidelines**: Alias for `/lazy-commit -f --qa-guideline --deploy-guideline --skip-graphify` — commit all changes on the current branch immediately, without the approval question, adding the `QA:` and `Deploy:` lists to the body of each commit whose type releases and leaving `graphify-out/` uncommitted. Use only when the user runs /lazy-commit-guidelines. ([wachirawitc/lazy-commit](https://github.com/wachirawitc/lazy-commit/tree/HEAD/lazy-commit-guidelines))
- **lazy-commit-no-graphify**: Alias for `/lazy-commit -f --skip-graphify` — commit all changes on the current branch immediately, without the approval question, leaving `graphify-out/` uncommitted. Use only when the user runs /lazy-commit-no-graphify. ([wachirawitc/lazy-commit](https://github.com/wachirawitc/lazy-commit/tree/HEAD/lazy-commit-no-graphify))
